Compute gradient of c array efficiently? For smoothed objective over all lags with weights w_m (sum weights=1), objective = dx * ∑ w_m c_m. Gradient = dx*∑ w_m grad(c_m). - For lag ℓ=index-q (q=n-1). If m>=0 (ℓ=q+m): c_m = ∑_{i=0}^{n-1-m} h_{i+m}(1-h_i). Derivative wrt h_i: from term as h_{i+m}: (1-h_i) for i=0..L-1; from term as 1-h_i: -h_{i+m} for i=0..L-1? Wait derivative of ∑ h_{i+m} - h_{i+m}h_i:
∂/∂h_p:
if p = i (second factor, i from 0..L-1): -(h_{p+m}) (if p+m<n)
if p = i+m (first factor, i=p-m if 0≤i<L): (1-h_{p-m})
So vector grad length n: for m≥0 offset +m:
for i=0..L-1: grad[i] -= h[i+m]; grad[i+m] += 1-h[i].
For m<0, ℓ=-m? derivative symmetric: c_{-m}=∑_{i=m}^{n-1} h_{i-m}(1-h_i); For p: as shifted h_i? We can use previous coordinate derivative: for full index ms, if ms>=q (positive lag m=ms-q): a[p] = (valid_f? 1 - h[p+m]) + (valid_b? -h[p-m?])? Previous for variable p: if ms = p+m+q? Hmm.

Pseudo:
def optimize_smooth_topk(h, n, budget, Ks, lr, ...):
  t=time; m=n; best
  maintain velocity, var (Adam) size n.
  for iter:
    c=correlate(h,1-h)
    C=max c*dx
    weights = topK softmax? Use alpha_w = 50? Or simply K largest: weights uniform over top K_m. To smooth and focus, use topK with temperature: shifted = c - max; weights = exp(temperature*shifted); normalize over all? That's logsumexp. Top-K with uniform is less smooth at boundaries but okay. We can combine: keep K = int(10 + 200*progress), choose top K lags.
    grad = zero; for each top lag index, add derivative * weight.
    gradient of objective w.r.t h = dx * grad.
    Adam: u = beta1*u+(1-beta1)*g; v=...; p = -u/(sqrt(v)+eps)
    Need maintain sum: p = p - p.mean() (or adjust). But box clipping after step breaks sum; projection_feasible will adjust all. Use small lr.
    h_new = h + lr*p; project_feasible.
    line search: maybe compute C at h_new; if not improve, reduce lr? Could just do fixed. But nonmonotonic Adam can wander. Use a restart schedule: after each phase, coordinate descent.
  coordinate_cd every few hundred iters.

Now top-K Adam optimizer. Let's design in detail.

We need compute weighted gradient. Function add_lag_grad(grad, s, h, n): s full index.
  q=n-1; m=s-q
  if m>=0:
    L=n-m
    # i 0..L-1:
    # grad[i] -= h[i+m]
    # grad[i+m] += 1-h[i]
    grad[:L] -= h[m:]
    grad[m:m+L] += 1.0 - h[:L]
  else:
    a=-m
    L=n-a
    # i a..n-1: grad[i] -= h[i-a]
    # grad[i-a] += 1-h[i]
    grad[a:] -= h[:-a]
    grad[:-a] += 1.0 - h[a:]
  (Check m=0: L=n; grad[:n]-=h; grad[0:n]+=1-h => 1-2h)
This is vectorized O(n) per lag. If K=50, 25k ops; fine.

Standard isotonic? We need project vector onto [0,1] with sum target. Can solve by thresholding: find λ such that sum(clip(x+λ,0,1))=target. Monotone in λ. We can implement fast water-fill:
def project_fast(x,target):
  x=np.clip(x,0,1)
  s=x.sum()
  diff=target-s
  if abs(diff)<1e-12: return x
  if diff>0:
    # add to values with room. Sort by 1-x descending (i.e. x ascending). Greedily raise until target.
  else:
    # lower values, sort by x descending.
Use np.partition? For small n, loops over sorted indices. Since target changes by small, maybe one loop.
Algorithm for diff>0:
  cap=1-x. Need add diff proportional? To minimize L2, raise lowest x first (largest room) but proportional? Actually projection onto box+sum: x + min(λ,1-x)-max(λ,0) where λ scalar. Can find λ.

Pseudo for adam_topk:
  h = h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5
  m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); lr=start
  for T/K in schedule with iters:
    for it in range(iters):
      c = np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
      # weights? If K changes, topK
      idxs = top_K(c,K)
      g = np.zeros(n)
      for s in idxs: add_lag_grad(g,s,h,n)
      g /= len(idxs)
      # Adam
      m = b1*m+(1-b1)*g; v=b2*v+(1-b2)*g*g
      step = -m/(sqrt(v_hat)+eps)
      step -= np.mean(step)
      # optionally scaling: step = step / (np.abs(step).max()+1e-8) * max_step
      # choose max_step maybe 0.2 (h units). Adam step size can be large; clip.
      step = np.clip(step, -max_step, max_step)
      h_new = project(h + lr*step) ? lr then clip redundant; maybe h_new=project(h+step_scale*sign?)
      c_new = compute C
      if c_new < best_c: update best.
      # But maybe always accept if not too worse? h=h_new; if c_new > current*1.05, h=h; lr*=0.8; else maybe h=h_new.
    coordinate_cd few passes; update best.
Return best_h,best_c.

Let's detail add_lag_grad function with full index s from 0 to 2n-2.
We can compute with slices but be careful for m=0.
```
def add_lag_grad(out, s, h, n):
    q = n-1
    m = s - q
    if m >= 0:
        L = n - m
        if L <= 0: return
        out[:L] -= h[m:m+L]
        out[m:m+L] += 1.0 - h[:L]
    else:
        a = -m
        L = n - a
        if L <= 0: return
        out[a:a+L] -= h[:L]
        out[:L] += 1.0 - h[a:a+L]
```
Check negative m=-a: c=∑_{i=a}^{n-1} h_{i-a}(1-h_i). Let i=a..n-1, j=i-a. For out[j] (as first factor h_j): terms -h_j h_i => derivative wrt h_j = -h_{j+a}; out[j] -= h[j+a] for j=0..n-a-1 -> out[:L] -= h[a:a+L]. For out[i] (as second): derivative = 1 - h_{i-a}; out[a:] += 1-h[:L]. Our code: out[a:a+L] -= h[:L]; out[:L] += 1-h[a:a+L]. This differs indexing? Let's derive: out[i] for i=a..n-1 should += 1-h[i-a]. i-a ranges 0..L-1. So out[a:] += 1 - h[:L]. We should set out[a:a+L] += 1.0 - h[:L]. And out[:L] -= h[a:a+L]. Code above has reversed: out[a:] -= h[:L]; out[:L] +=1-h[a:]. That's for m positive? Need correct.
Let's do carefully:
Case m=-a:
 c = ∑_{i=a}^{n-1} h_{i-a}(1-h_i)
 For variable p:
 - as shifted (first) p = j, where i=j+a, j=0..L-1. derivative = -h_{j+a} = -h[p+a]. So out[j] -= h[j+a] -> out[:L] -= h[a:]
 - as unshifted (second) p = i, where j=i-a, i=a..n-1. derivative = 1 - h_{i-a} = 1 - h[p-a]. So out[i] += 1 - h[i-a] -> out[a:] += 1 - h[:L]
Thus add:
  out[:L] -= h[a:a+L]
  out[a:a+L] += 1.0 - h[:L]
Check m=0: L=n, both apply: out[:n]-=h; out[:n]+=1-h =>1-2h. Good.
For positive m:
 c = ∑_{i=0}^{n-1-m} h_{i+m}(1-h_i)
 as first p=j=i+m -> out[j] += 1-h[j-m] (j=m..n-1) -> out[m:] += 1-h[:L]
 as second p=i -> out[i] -= h[i+m] (i=0..L-1) -> out[:L] -= h[m:]
Yes code for m>=0: out[:L] -= h[m:]; out[m:] += 1.0 - h[:L]. Good.

M = max(p, n-1-p)

  a[cond2] -= h[p + m_arr[cond2]]

```
def adam_topk(h,n,deadline, max_iters=10000, Kschedule=[20,50,100,200], lr0=0.2, beta1=0.9,beta2=0.99):
    h = project...
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5
    m_s=np.zeros(n); v_s=np.zeros(n)
    lr=lr0
    for K in Kschedule:
       iters_per_K = max(500, max_iters//len(Kschedule))
       for it in range(iters_per_K):
          if time > deadline: break
          c = np.correlate(h,1-h,full)
          # K indices
          Kcur = min(K, len(c))
          if Kcur == len(c): idx=all
          else: idx = np.argpartition(c, -Kcur)[-Kcur:]
          g = np.zeros(n)
          for s in idx:
              add_lag_grad(g, int(s), h, n)
          g *= 1.0/Kcur
          # maybe subtract mean to keep sum? Adam step zero mean later.
          m_s = ...
          v_s = ...
          step = -m_hat/(sqrt(v_hat)+1e-8)
          step = step - np.mean(step)
          # rescale step: if max(abs(step))>max_step, scale
          max_step=0.2
          sgn=np.max(np.abs(step))
          if sgn>max_step: step *= max_step/sgn
          h_new = project_exact(h + step, n/2)  # no lr? Adam step already scale; maybe use lr=1 with max_step. 
          # But if step max 0.2 too large? Use lr multiplier: step=lr*step, lr<=1.
```

import numpy as np
import time

def compute_c5(h,n):
    dx=2.0/n
    c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
    return float(np.max(c)*dx)

def project_exact(h,target):
    h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    s=float(h.sum())
    if abs(s-target) < 1e-12: return h
    if s>target:
        lo=0.0; hi=1.0 # subtract lambda
        def f? no closures. Could implement loop with inlined computation.
        for _ in range(40):
            lam=0.5*(lo+hi)
            ys=np.maximum(h-lam,0.0)
            if ys.sum() > target: lo=lam
            else: hi=lam
        lam=0.5*(lo+hi)
        return np.clip(h-lam,0.0,1.0)
    else:
        lam=0.0; hi=1.0
        for _ in range(40):
           mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
           ys=np.minimum(h+mid,1.0)
           if ys.sum() < target: lo=mid
           else: hi=mid
        lam=0.5*(lo+hi)
        return np.clip(h+lam,0.0,1.0)
```

    h=np.clip(h,0,1)

def get_offsets(n):
    global OFFSET_CACHE
    if OFFSET_CACHE is not None and OFFSET_CACHE[0]==n:
       return OFFSET_CACHE[1]
    q=n-1
    mlist=[]; slist=[]

       m=np.arange(-M,M+1, dtype=int)
       s=(q+m).astype(int)

Coordinate_cd pass:
```
def coordinate_cd(h,n,max_passes,deadline,grid=96,refine=192):
  h=h.copy(); q=n-1; mlist,slist=get_offsets(n)
  dx=2/n
  c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
  best_h=h.copy(); best_c=float(c.max()*dx)
  for pass in range(max_passes):
    if time.time()>deadline: break
    current_max=float(c.max())
    improved=False
    for p in range(n):
      if time.time()>deadline: break
      d,val = coord_step(...)
      if d !=0:
        improved=True; current_max=val
    if improved:
      h=project_exact(h,n/2.0)
      c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
      nc=float(c.max()*dx)
      if nc < best_c:
          best_c=nc; best_h=h.copy()
    else:
      break
  return best_h,best_c
```

def topk_indices(c,K):
    K=int(K)
    if K>=c.size: return np.arange(c.size)
    # maybe ensure K positive
    # argpartition returning unsorted top K
    out=np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
    return out
```

def line_search_dir(h_dir_base, d, n, Cbase, ts):
   best_h=h_base; bestC=Cbase
   if np.max(np.abs(d))==0 return
   d=d/np.max(np.abs(d)); d-=d.mean()
   for t in ts:
      ht=project_exact(h+t*d, n/2)
      ch=compute_c5(ht,n)
      if ch<bestC: bestH=ht; bestC=ch
   return bestH,bestC
```

def subgradient_local(h,n,deadline,max_trials=1000):
   h=h.copy(); target=n/2; dx=2/n
   c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'); C=float(c.max()*dx); best=...
   ts=[0.05,0.03,0.02,0.01,0.005]
   Kmodes=[(5,'all'),(15,'all'),(15,'pos'),(15,'neg'),(30,'all')]
   for it in range(max_trials):
      if time>deadline: break
      c = correlate; C=...
      if C<best: update
      improved=False
      for K,mode in Kmodes:
          if time>deadline: break
          scores=c
          offset=0
          if mode=='pos': scores=c[q+1:]; offset=q+1; K=min(K, len)
          elif mode=='neg': scores=c[:q]; offset=0; K=min(K,q)
          if len(scores)<2: continue
          idx=topk_indices(scores,K)+offset
          g=zeros
          for s in idx: add_lag_grad(g,int(s),h,n)
          g /= len(idx)
          d = -g
          h2,C2=line_search_dir(h,d,n,C,ts)
          if C2 < C - 1e-12:
              h,C=h2,C2; improved=True; break
      if not improved:
          # try one smaller random top? maybe break after several tries
          break
   return h,bestC  # but h may not be best? track best_h,bestC
```
Need if accepted, consume? Continue. If a K direction improves by tiny, next iteration. If no direction improves, maybe coordinate next.

def apply_single_delta(h,c,p,n,delta,m_arr,s_arr):
   if delta ==0: return c.max()
   h0=h[p]

   a=... using h (old)

   h[p]=hn

Binary improve function:

def binary_improve(b,n,max_trials,deadline,rng):
   b=b.copy()
   target=n//2?
   ones=[...] zeros=[...]
   c=correlate...
   bestC=max*dx; best_b=b.copy()
   mlist,slist=get_offsets(n)
   noimp=0
   for t in range(max_trials):
      if time>deadline break
      i=ones[rng.randint(len(ones))]; j=zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))]
      # eval
      apply_single_delta(b,c,i,n,-1.0,mlist[i],slist[i])
      # now b_i=0
      apply_single_delta(b,c,j,n,+1.0,mlist[j],slist[j])
      emax=float(c.max())
      if emax < best_units - 1e-12: # best_units = bestC/dx
         accept: update best, update ones zeros, noimp=0
      else:
         # rollback
         apply_single_delta(b,c,j,n,-1.0,mlist[j],slist[j])
         apply_single_delta(b,c,i,n,+1.0,mlist[i],slist[i])
         noimp+=1
         if noimp>5000: break
   return best_b,bestC
```

```
def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
    t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+budget_s-6
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    # load/choose n
    ...
    best_h=project...
    best_c=compute_c5
    # Phase 1 coord initial
    best_h,best_c = coordinate_cd(best_h,n,80,deadline,grid=96,refine=192)
    # Phase 2 subgradient
    if time<deadline-60:
        hs,cs=subgradient_local(best_h,n,deadline, max_trials=1500)
        if cs<best_c: update
        hs,cs=coordinate_cd(hs,n,60,deadline,grid=128,refine=240)
        if cs<best_c: update
    # Phase 3 binary
    if time<deadline-120:
       b=binarize(best_h,n)
       # run binary_improve limited max_trials 20000 but deadline min(deadline-80)
       b2,bc2=binary_improve(b,n,20000,deadline-80,rng)
       if bc2<best_c:
          best_h,best_c=b2.astype(float),bc2
          # coordinate refine
          best_h,best_c=coordinate_cd(best_h,n,80,deadline-20,grid=128,refine=256)
    # Phase 4 subgradient again
    if time<deadline-80:
       hs,cs=subgradient_local(best_h,n,deadline, max_trials=800)
       if cs<best_c: update
       hs,cs=coordinate_cd(hs,n,60,deadline-40)
       if cs<best_c: update
    # Phase 5 perturb restarts
    if time<deadline-120:
       for sigma in [0.03,0.06,0.1]:
           hp=project_exact(best_h + rng.normal(0,sigma,n), n/2)
           hc,cc=coordinate_cd(hp,n,40,deadline-60,grid=80,refine=128)
           if cc<best_c:
               hc,cs=subgradient_local(hc,n,deadline,max_trials=200)
               if cs<cc: cc=cs; hc=hs
               hc,cc=coordinate_cd(hc,n,40,deadline-40,grid=128,refine=200)
               if cc<best_c: update
           if time>deadline-60: break
    # Phase 6 structured/resample? optional
    if time<deadline-120:
       for kind in [0,1,2,3]:
          hs=structured_start(n,kind,rng)
          hc,cc=coordinate_cd(hs,n,40,deadline-60,grid=80,refine=128)
          if cc<best_c: update maybe light subgrad
          if time>deadline-80: break
    # Final exact coordinate polish
    if time<deadline-30:
       best_h,best_c=coordinate_cd(best_h,n,100,deadline-5,grid=200,refine=400)
    # Final small subgradient?
    if time<deadline-10:
       hs,cs=subgradient_local(best_h,n,deadline,max_trials=100, quick?)
       if cs<best_c: update
    best_h=project_exact(best_h,n/2)
    best_c=compute_c5(best_h,n)
    return best_h,best_c,n
```

1. `project_exact` branches:
```
def project_exact(h, target):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    s = float(h.sum())
    if abs(s-target) < 1e-13:
       return h
    if s > target:
       lo=0.0; hi=1.0
       for _ in range(35):
          mid=(lo+hi)*0.5
          val=float(np.maximum(h-mid,0.0).sum())
          if val > target: lo=mid
          else: hi=mid
       lam=0.5*(lo+hi)
       h = np.maximum(h - lam, 0.0)
    else:
       lo=0.0; hi=1.0
       for _ in range(35):
          mid=(lo+hi)*0.5
          val=float(np.minimum(h+mid,1.0).sum())
          if val < target: lo=mid
          else: hi=mid
       lam=0.5*(lo+hi)
       h = np.minimum(h + lam, 1.0)
    # exact adjust
    diff = target - float(h.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
       # distribute among unsaturated
       if diff > 0:
          mask = h < 1.0
       else: mask = h > 0.0
       if mask.any():
          h[mask] += diff / int(mask.sum())
          h = np.clip(h,0,1)
          # if still diff, adjust one
          diff2 = target - float(h.sum())
          if abs(diff2)>1e-14:
             if diff2>0:
                idx=int(np.argmin(1.0-h)); h[idx]=min(1.0,h[idx]+diff2)
             else:
                idx=int(np.argmax(h)); h[idx]=max(0.0,h[idx]+diff2)
    return h
```
The distribute diff/mask.sum may exceed bounds if diff large and mask full? But diff small because bisection. If not small (e.g. binary bisection hi=1 not enough? For s>target but target close n, subtract lambda up to1 can reach 0. Bisection target maybe >n? target=n/2 in (0,n). ok. For increase, target maybe n? h all? ok.
If diff distribution splits among many and some saturate, final adjust may not fully fix if diff large. But bisection enough.

2. compute_c5 uses `np.max(c)`; c array dtype float. dx=2.0/n.

3. get_offsets: Need global `_OFFSET_CACHE`. Avoid list of Python ints? arrays dtype int. Good.
```
_OFFSET_CACHE = (None, None)
def get_offsets(n):
    if _OFFSET_CACHE[0] == n: return _OFFSET_CACHE[1]
    q=n-1; ms=[]; ss=[]
    for p in range(n):
      M=p if p>n-1-p else n-1-p
      m=np.arange(-M,M+1,dtype=int)
      s=q+m
      ms.append(m); ss.append(s)
    _OFFSET_CACHE=(n,(ms,ss))
    return _OFFSET_CACHE[1]
```
Need global assignment: declare `global _OFFSET_CACHE` in function.

4. `coord_step` uses m_arr, s_arr. Need be careful if `d_best` negative, `h[p]` clip. We update c with `best_d`. But if `h0+best_d` after clipping differs due to boundaries? step only within [lo,hi], so no clip needed except round.

Implementation of vector a in coord_step:
```
a = np.zeros(len(m_arr), dtype=float)
pos = m_arr >= 0
if np.any(pos):
    idx = p + m_arr
    valid = pos & (idx < n)
    a[valid] -= h[idx[valid]]
    idx1 = p - m_arr
    valid1 = pos & (idx1 >= 0)
    a[valid1] += 1.0 - h[idx1[valid1]]
neg = m_arr < 0
if np.any(neg):
    ab = -m_arr
    idx2 = p + ab
    valid2 = neg & (idx2 < n)
    a[valid2] -= h[idx2[valid2]]
    idxb = p - ab
    validb = neg & (p >= ab)
    a[validb] += 1.0 - h[idxb[validb]]
```
For neg, idxb = p - ab; valid p>=ab ensures idxb>=0. Good. For m=0, pos includes; neg false. Fine.

```
def top_subset(c, K, mode, q):
   if mode=='pos': sc=c[q+1:]; off=q+1
   elif mode=='neg': sc=c[:q]; off=0
   else: sc=c; off=0
   Kmin=min(K, sc.size)
   if Kmin<=0: return None
   idx=np.argpartition(sc, -Kmin)[-Kmin:]
   return idx+off
```

10. line_search_dir: d might be integer? Convert float. Need project_exact target. Use `compute_c5` each trial. Could optimize by passing dx but okay.
```
def line_search_dir(h_base, d, n, Cbase):
    besth=h_base; bestC=Cbase
    d=np.asarray(d,float).copy()
    nm=np.max(np.abs(d))
    if nm < 1e-14: return besth,bestC
    d = d/nm
    d = d - np.mean(d)
    ts = (0.08,0.04,0.02,0.01,0.004)
    for t in ts:
       ht=project_exact(h_base + t*d, n/2.0)
       ch=compute_c5(ht,n)
       if ch < bestC:
          besth=ht; bestC=ch
    return besth,bestC
```

Binarize:
```
def binarize(h,n):
   b=np.zeros(n)
   target=n//2
   if target==0: return b
   order=np.argsort(h, kind='mergesort')
   b[order[-target:]] = 1.0
   return b
```
If n even target n/2. If n odd, n//2 not target n/2; binary cannot exact. For binary, use floor. But if n odd, skip. In run if n%2==0.

13. structured_start: Need no long literals. Use array formulas.
```
def structured_start(n, kind, rng):
   x=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n,endpoint=False)
   if kind==0:
      h=0.5 + 0.45*np.sin(np.pi*x) + 0.05*np.sin(2*np.pi*x)
   elif kind==1:
      h=0.5 + 0.45*np.sin(2*np.pi*x)
   elif kind==2:
      h=0.5 + 0.4*np.cos(np.pi*x)
   elif kind==3:
      h=(np.sin(3*np.pi*x) > 0.0).astype(float)*0.5 + 0.25  # not zero mean? project
   else:
      h=rng.uniform(0.0,1.0,n)
   return project_exact(h, n/2.0)
```
Good.

Pseudo run more detailed:
```
def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
   t_start=time.time(); deadline=t_start+budget_s-5
   rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
   # load
   best... bestC
   alloc1=t_start+0.22*budget_s
   best_h,best_c = coordinate_cd(best_h,n,max_passes=120,deadline=alloc1,grid=56,refine=144)
   alloc2=t_start+0.45*budget_s
   if time<alloc2-30:
      hs,cs=subgradient_local(best_h,n,alloc2, max_trials=2000)
      if cs<best_c: best_h,best_c=hs,cs
      hs,cs=coordinate_cd(best_h,n,60,alloc2,grid=72,refine=160)
      if cs<best_c: update
   alloc3=t_start+0.65*budget_s
   if n%2==0 and time<alloc3-60:
       b=binarize(best_h,n)
       b2,bc2=binary_improve(b,n,30000,alloc3,rng)
       if bc2<best_c:
          best_h,best_c=b2,bc2
          best_h,best_c=coordinate_cd(best_h,n,50,alloc3,grid=72,refine=160)
   alloc4=t_start+0.80*budget_s
   if time<alloc4-40:
      hs,cs=subgradient_local(best_h,n,alloc4, max_trials=1500)
      if cs<best_c: update
      hs,cs=coordinate_cd(best_h,n,50,alloc4,grid=80,refine=180)
      if cs<best_c: update
   alloc5=t_start+0.95*budget_s
   if time<alloc5-30:
      # local perturb/stoch
      for sigma in (0.02,0.05,0.08):
         if time>alloc5-30: break
         hp=project_exact(best_h+rng.normal(0,sigma,n), n/2)
         hc,cc=coordinate_cd(hp,n,30,alloc5-20,grid=64,refine=144)
         if cc<best_c:
            hs,cs=subgradient_local(hc,n,alloc5-10,max_trials=150)
            if cs<cc: hc,cc=hs,cs
            hc,cc=coordinate_cd(hc,n,30,alloc5-10,grid=80,refine=160)
            if cc<best_c: best_h,best_c=hc,cc
      # structured restarts if time
      if time<alloc5-60:
        for kind in range(4):
           if time>alloc5-40: break
           hs=structured_start(n,kind,rng)
           hc,cc=coordinate_cd(hs,n,30,alloc5-20,grid=64,refine=144)
           if cc<best_c:
              hs,cs=subgradient_local(hc,n,alloc5-10,max_trials=100)
              if cs<cc: cc=cs
              hc,cc=coordinate_cd(hc,n,30,alloc5-10,grid=80,refine=160)
              if cc<best_c: best_h,best_c=hc,cc
   # final polish
   best_h,best_c=coordinate_cd(best_h,n,80,deadline,grid=96,refine=256)
   if time<deadline-5:
      hs,cs=subgradient_local(best_h,n,deadline-2,max_trials=200)
      if cs<best_c: best_h,best_c=hs,cs
      best_h,best_c=coordinate_cd(best_h,n,50,deadline,grid=128,refine=300)
   best_h=project_exact(best_h,n/2.0)
   best_c=compute_c5(best_h,n)
   return best_h,best_c,n
```

   a[valid] -= h[idx[valid]]  # positive second? Wait for positive: second p+m? yes

   a[validf] += 1.0 - h[idxf[validf]]
   # second: p>=ab -> -h[p-ab]
   idxs = p - ab
   valids = neg & (p >= ab)
   a[valids] -= h[idxs[valids]]
return a
```

```
def select_indices(c, K, mode, q, rng):
    if mode == 'pos':
       sc = c[q+1:]; off = q+1
    elif mode == 'neg':
       sc = c[:q]; off = 0
    else:
       sc = c; off = 0
    K = min(int(K), sc.size)
    if K <= 0: return None
    if mode != 'rand':
       Kb = K
    else:
       Kb = min(3*K, sc.size)
    idx = topk_indices(sc, Kb) + off
    if mode == 'rand' and idx.size > K:
       sel = rng.choice(idx.size, K, replace=False)
       idx = idx[sel]
    return idx
```

Let's draft full code now in analysis, check for syntax.

```
import numpy as np
import time

_OFFSET_CACHE = (None, None)

def compute_c5(h, n):
    dx = 2.0 / n
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    return float(np.max(c) * dx)

def project_exact(h, target):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).ravel().copy()
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    s = float(h.sum())
    if abs(s - target) < 1e-13:
        return h
    if s > target:
        lo = 0.0; hi = 1.0
        for _ in range(40):
            mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
            val = float(np.maximum(h - mid, 0.0).sum())
            if val > target:
                lo = mid
            else:
                hi = mid
        lam = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        h = np.maximum(h - lam, 0.0)
    else:
        lo = 0.0; hi = 1.0
        for _ in range(40):
            mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
            val = float(np.minimum(h + mid, 1.0).sum())
            if val < target:
                lo = mid
            else:
                hi = mid
        lam = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        h = np.minimum(h + lam, 1.0)
    diff = target - float(h.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
        if diff > 0:
            mask = h < 1.0
        else:
            mask = h > 0.0
        cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
        if cnt > 0:
            h[mask] += diff / cnt
            h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
        diff = target - float(h.sum())
        if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
            if diff > 0:
                idx = int(np.argmin(1.0 - h))
                h[idx] = min(1.0, h[idx] + diff)
            else:
                idx = int(np.argmax(h))
                h[idx] = max(0.0, h[idx] + diff)
    return h
```
`np.argmin(1.0-h)` if h all 1 and diff>0 impossible because target n/2. Good.

get_offsets:
```
def get_offsets(n):
    global _OFFSET_CACHE
    if _OFFSET_CACHE[0] == n:
        return _OFFSET_CACHE[1]
    q = n - 1
    infos = []
    for p in range(n):
        M = p if p > n - 1 - p else n - 1 - p
        m = np.arange(-M, M + 1, dtype=int)
        pos = m >= 0
        neg = m < 0
        ab = -m
        lo = q - M
        hi = q + M
        infos.append((lo, hi, m, pos, neg, ab, M))
    _OFFSET_CACHE = (n, infos)
    return infos
```
For m=0, ab=0. pos true, neg false. Good.

coord_derivative:
```
def coord_derivative(h, p, n, info):
    lo, hi, m, pos, neg, ab, diag = info
    L = hi - lo + 1
    a = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
    if np.any(pos):
        idx = p + m
        valid = pos & (idx < n)
        if np.any(valid):
            a[valid] -= h[idx[valid]]
        idx1 = p - m
        valid1 = pos & (idx1 >= 0)
        if np.any(valid1):
            a[valid1] += 1.0 - h[idx1[valid1]]
    if np.any(neg):
        idxf = p + ab
        validf = neg & (idxf < n)
        if np.any(validf):
            a[validf] += 1.0 - h[idxf[validf]]
        idxs = p - ab
        valids = neg & (p >= ab)
        if np.any(valids):
            a[valids] -= h[idxs[valids]]
    return a
```
For m=0 pos: idx=p, valid p<n true => a-=h[p]; idx1=p, valid1=> a+=1-h[p]; total 1-2h. Good.
For positive m, `idx=p+m` may be >=n, valid false. `idx1=p-m` can be negative, valid false. Good.
For negative, ab positive; `p >= ab` works with ab array: valids = neg & (p >= ab) -> bool array. `idxs=p-ab`, for valid indices nonnegative. Good.

apply_delta:
```
def apply_delta(h, c, p, delta, n, info):
    lo, hi, m, pos, neg, ab, diag = info
    h0 = h[p]
    hn = min(1.0, max(0.0, h0 + delta))
    actual = hn - h0
    if abs(actual) < 1e-15:
        return float(np.max(c))
    a = coord_derivative(h, p, n, info)
    c[lo:hi+1] += actual * a
    q = n - 1
    c[q] -= actual * actual
    h[p] = hn
    return float(np.max(c))
```
`c` is array, modified. If p's affected slice does not include q? It always does because M>=0 includes m=0 q in lo..hi. Good.

coord_step:
```
def coord_step(h, c, p, n, info, current_max, coarse, refine):
    lo, hi, m, pos, neg, ab, diag = info
    base = c[lo:hi+1]
    unaff = 0.0
    if lo > 0:
        unaff = float(np.max(c[:lo]))
    if hi < 2 * n - 2:
        v = float(np.max(c[hi+1:]))
        if v > unaff: unaff = v
    if current_max < unaff: current_max = unaff
    a = coord_derivative(h, p, n, info)
    h0 = h[p]
    lo_d = -h0
    hi_d = 1.0 - h0
    span = hi_d - lo_d
    if span < 1e-14:
        return 0.0, current_max
    # if all a and diag? Could skip
    D = np.linspace(lo_d, hi_d, coarse)
    vals = base[None, :] + D[:, None] * a
    d2 = D[:, None] * D[:, None]
    vals[:, diag] -= d2
    row = vals.max(axis=1)
    if unaff > 0.0:
        row = np.maximum(row, unaff)
    bi = int(np.argmin(row))
    best_val = float(row[bi])
    best_d = float(D[bi])
    if best_val < current_max - 1e-12:
        cell = span / max(1, coarse - 1)
        rad = max(cell * 2.0, span / 100.0)
        rlo = max(lo_d, best_d - rad)
        rhi = min(hi_d, best_d + rad)
        if rhi > rlo + 1e-15:
            D2 = np.linspace(rlo, rhi, refine)
            vals2 = base[None, :] + D2[:, None] * a
            vals2[:, diag] -= D2[:, None] * D2[:, None]
            row2 = vals2.max(axis=1)
            row2 = np.maximum(row2, unaff)
            bi2 = int(np.argmin(row2))
            if row2[bi2] < best_val - 1e-13:
                best_val = float(row2[bi2]); best_d = float(D2[bi2])
        # if best_d == 0? no refine? We only refine if best_val < current; if best_d maybe 0? But if D grid no 0, best_d could not be 0 unless row at endpoint? Could still. If best_d=0 and best_val<current, refine around 0. rad ok.
    elif span > 1e-8 and (not D.size or abs(D[0]) > 1e-12 or abs(D[-1]) > 1e-12?): # maybe need fine around 0 if coarse didn't include?
        pass
    if abs(best_d) > 1e-14 and best_val < current_max - 1e-12:
        hn = min(1.0, max(0.0, h0 + best_d))
        actual = hn - h0
        if abs(actual) > 1e-14:
            c[lo:hi+1] += actual * a
            q = n - 1
            c[q] -= actual * actual
            h[p] = hn
        return actual, best_val
    return 0.0, current_max
```
Issue: `D = np.linspace(lo_d, hi_d, coarse)`. If coarse small, may not include 0. best_d may be endpoint. If coarse grid finds improvement, refine. If no improvement, no. Good.
But if `coarse` is 20, D doesn't include 0; best_val is maybe > current_max? We initialize best_val row min, but if all rows >current_max, best_val >current_max; no update. Good.
However if row has value less than current but coarse cell around 0 not present but some other present; refine. Good.
If best_d from coarse is 0? possible only if linspace includes 0; but coarse not too. If best_val < current and best_d not 0, refine. If best_d 0? no, D doesn't include 0 unless span multiple and coarse odd could. The elif unused. We can remove.
When refining, `rad = max(cell*2, span/100)`. If best at endpoint, rad clipped. Good.
`d2 = D[:,None]*D[:,None]` memory; if diag = M, `vals[:, diag]` broadcasting d2 shape (coarse,1) subtract works? In numpy, `vals[:, diag]` shape (coarse,1), `d2` shape (coarse,1). Good. For refine same.
Potential issue: If `coarse` = 1? We set at least 24. Good.
`a` may contain nan? No.

Coordinate_cd:
```
def coordinate_cd(h, n, max_passes, deadline, coarse=48, refine=144, max_drift=3.0):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).ravel().copy()
    infos = get_offsets(n)
    q = n - 1
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    dx = 2.0 / n
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = float(np.max(c) * dx)
    target = n / 2.0
    for _ in range(max_passes):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
        current_max = float(np.max(c))
        drift = 0.0
        improved = False
        for p in range(n):
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break
            d, new_max = coord_step(h, c, p, n, infos[p], current_max, coarse, refine)
            if d != 0.0:
                improved = True
                current_max = new_max
                drift += d
                if abs(drift) > max_drift:
                    h = project_exact(h, target)
                    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
                    current_max = float(np.max(c))
                    drift = 0.0
        if improved:
            h = project_exact(h, target)
            c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
            nc = float(np.max(c) * dx)
            if nc < best_c - 1e-14:
                best_c = nc
                best_h = h.copy()
            # continue with projected state
        else:
            break
    if float(np.max(np.correlate(best_h, 1-best_h, mode='full')) * dx) < best_c:
        best_c = compute_c5(best_h,n)
    return best_h, best_c
```
At start pass, c recomputed; after coordinate loop, project. If no improvement in pass but c maybe slightly changed due to previous pass? best already. Break.
`drift` after mid projection resets but h projected; current_max recalculated. Good.
Potential: If mid projection occurs, the remaining coordinates in pass use projected h/c. `improved` already true; final project at pass. Good.

add_lag_grad:
```
def add_lag_grad(out, s, h, n):
    q = n - 1
    m = int(s) - q
    if m >= 0:
        L = n - m
        if L <= 0: return
        out[:L] -= h[m:m+L]
        out[m:m+L] += 1.0 - h[:L]
    else:
        a = -m
        L = n - a
        if L <= 0: return
        out[:L] += 1.0 - h[a:a+L]
        out[a:a+L] -= h[:L]
```
Check negative with corrected signs: out[:L] += first; out[a:a+L]-=second. Good.

topk_indices:
```
def topk_indices(scores, K):
    K = int(K)
    if K <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=int)
    if K >= scores.size:
        return np.arange(scores.size, dtype=int)
    return np.argpartition(scores, -K)[-K:].astype(int)
```

select_indices:
```
def select_indices(c, K, mode, q, rng):
    if mode == 'pos':
        sc = c[q+1:]
        off = q + 1
    elif mode == 'neg':
        sc = c[:q]
        off = 0
    else:
        sc = c
        off = 0
    K = min(int(K), sc.size)
    if K <= 0:
        return None
    Kb = K if mode != 'rand' else min(3 * K, sc.size)
    idx = topk_indices(sc, Kb)
    if idx.size == 0: return None
    idx = idx + off
    if mode == 'rand' and idx.size > K:
        sel = rng.choice(idx.size, K, replace=False)
        idx = idx[sel]
    return idx
```
If mode pos and q+1 out? n>1. Good.

line_search_dir:
```
def line_search_dir(h_base, d, n, Cbase):
    best_h = h_base
    best_c = Cbase
    d = np.asarray(d, dtype=float).ravel().copy()
    d -= np.mean(d)
    nm = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
    if nm < 1e-14:
        return best_h, best_c
    d /= nm
    ts = (0.08, 0.04, 0.02, 0.01, 0.004, 0.002)
    target = n / 2.0
    for t in ts:
        ht = project_exact(h_base + t * d, target)
        ch = compute_c5(ht, n)
        if ch < best_c:
            best_h = ht
            best_c = ch
    return best_h, best_c
```
If a larger t improves but smaller t not, we keep best. Good.

subgradient_local:
```
def subgradient_local(h, n, deadline, max_trials=1000, rng=None):
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.RandomState(12345)
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).ravel().copy()
    target = n / 2.0
    dx = 2.0 / n
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    best_c = float(np.max(c) * dx)
    best_h = h.copy()
    modes = ((3, 'all'), (3, 'pos'), (3, 'neg'), (10, 'all'), (10, 'pos'), (10, 'rand'), (25, 'all'), (25, 'rand'))
    stall = 0
    for it in range(max_trials):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
        C = float(np.max(c) * dx)
        if C < best_c - 1e-14:
            best_c = C; best_h = h.copy()
        q = n - 1
        trial_best = None
        trial_C = C
        for K, mode in modes:
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break
            idx = select_indices(c, K, mode, q, rng)
            if idx is None or idx.size == 0:
                continue
            g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
            for sidx in idx:
                add_lag_grad(g, int(sidx), h, n)
            g /= float(idx.size)
            h2, C2 = line_search_dir(h, -g, n, C)
            if C2 < trial_C - 1e-12:
                trial_C = C2
                trial_best = h2
        if trial_best is not None and trial_C < C - 1e-12:
            h = trial_best
            if C < best_c? wait trial_C < C and C maybe updated? Actually we accept if trial_C < C. If C < best_c, before acceptance best_h updated at start. good.
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall >= 3:
                break
    # final check
    cf = compute_c5(h,n)
    if cf < best_c:
        best_c = cf; best_h = h.copy()
    return best_h, best_c
```
Need if accepted but trial_C not lower than best_c? it is lower than C, and C at start was current, best_c<=C. Could still trial_C lower than best_c? yes update at next loop. Good.
We don't recompute c after acceptance inside same iteration; next. Good.
`stall` increments when all modes fail; if one fails? no. Break after 3 failed iterations. Good.
But in a failed iteration, `C` may be >best_c? We keep best_h. h remains at failed current. Good.

Could line_search_dir return `h_base` with best_c=C; then C2=C not < trial_C. Good.

Binary functions:
```
def binarize(h,n):
    b=np.zeros(n)
    target=n//2
    if target>0 and target<n:
        order=np.argsort(h, kind='mergesort')
        b[order[-target:]] = 1.0
    return b
```
If n odd, not exact. Skip.

binary_improve:
```
def binary_improve(b, n, max_trials, deadline, rng):
    b=np.asarray(b,float).ravel().copy()
    infos=get_offsets(n)
    c=np.correlate(b,1-b,mode='full')
    best_units=float(np.max(c))
    best_h=b.copy()
    ones=[i for i in range(n) if b[i] > 0.5]
    zeros=[i for i in range(n) if b[i] <= 0.5]
    if not ones or not zeros:
       return best_h, best_units * (2.0/n)
    noimp=0
    dx=2.0/n
    for t in range(max_trials):
        if time.time()>deadline: break
        i=ones[int(rng.randint(len(ones)))]
        j=zeros[int(rng.randint(len(zeros)))]
        if i == j: continue
        # eval swap in-place
        apply_delta(b,c,i,-1.0,n,infos[i])
        apply_delta(b,c,j,+1.0,n,infos[j])
        val=float(np.max(c))
        if val < best_units - 1e-12:
            # accept
            best_units=val
            best_h=b.copy()
            # update lists
            ones.remove(i)
            zeros.append(i)
            zeros.remove(j)
            ones.append(j)
            noimp=0
        else:
            # rollback
            apply_delta(b,c,j,-1.0,n,infos[j])
            apply_delta(b,c,i,+1.0,n,infos[i])
            noimp += 1
            if noimp > 10000:
                break
        if t % 4000 == 0:
            c=np.correlate(b,1-b,mode='full')
    cf=float(np.max(c)*dx)
    if cf < best_units*dx:
       best_units=cf; best_h=b.copy()
    return best_h, best_units*dx
```
List remove O(n). If noimp >10000 break, but max_trials maybe 30000. Good.
After rollback, c should be original; but due to rounding, recompute every 4000. However after rollback, c maybe not exact; subsequent swap evaluation based on slightly wrong c. Recomp periodically. Could recompute after rollback if noimp reaches 100? Maybe too expensive. Use if `t % 500 == 0`. 30000/500=60 correlates, fine. Set 1000.
Need ensure after accept, c is current; no recompute. Good.
If accept and then list operations: i in ones, j in zeros. `zeros.remove(j)` okay. `ones.append(j)`. If lists contain ints, okay.

structured_start:
```
def structured_start(n, kind, rng):
    x=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n,endpoint=False)
    if kind == 0:
       h=0.5 + 0.45*np.sin(np.pi*x) + 0.05*np.sin(2.0*np.pi*x)
    elif kind == 1:
       h=0.5 + 0.45*np.sin(2.0*np.pi*x) - 0.1*np.sin(4.0*np.pi*x)
    elif kind == 2:
       h=0.5 + 0.45*np.cos(np.pi*x)
    elif kind == 3:
       h=0.5 + 0.35*np.sin(3.0*np.pi*x) + 0.15*np.sin(np.pi*x)
    else:
       h=rng.uniform(0.0,1.0,n)
    return project_exact(h, n/2.0)
```

run:
Need handle initial length even. Code:
```
def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0=time.time()
    if budget_s > 60:
       deadline=t0 + budget_s - 0.03*budget_s
    else:
       deadline=t0 + 0.9*budget_s
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
       h=np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).ravel().copy()
    except Exception:
       h=None
    if h is None or h.size == 0:
       n=300
       h=structured_start(n,0,rng)
    else:
       n=int(h.size)
       if n > 800:
          n2=500
          x0=np.linspace(0,2,n,endpoint=False); x1=np.linspace(0,2,n2,endpoint=False)
          h=project_exact(np.interp(x1,x0,h), n2/2.0); n=n2
       if n % 2 == 1:
          n2 = max(2, n-1)
          x0=np.linspace(0,2,n,endpoint=False); x1=np.linspace(0,2,n2,endpoint=False)
          h=project_exact(np.interp(x1,x0,h), n2/2.0); n=n2
    target=n/2.0
    h=project_exact(h,target)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
    budget=float(budget_s)
    # phases
    ph1=t0+0.25*budget
    if ph1>deadline: ph1=deadline
    best_h,best_c=coordinate_cd(best_h,n,80,ph1,coarse=48,refine=128)
    ph2=t0+0.45*budget
    if ph2>deadline: ph2=deadline
    if time.time()<ph2-2:
       hs,cs=subgradient_local(best_h,n,ph2,max_trials=800,rng=rng)
       if cs<best_c: best_h,best_c=hs,cs
       hs,cs=coordinate_cd(best_h,n,50,ph2,coarse=56,refine=160)
       if cs<best_c: best_h,best_c=hs,cs
    ph3=t0+0.65*budget
    if ph3>deadline: ph3=deadline
    if n%2==0 and time.time()<ph3-30:
       b=binarize(best_h,n)
       b2,bc=binary_improve(b,n,30000,ph3,rng)
       if bc < best_c:
          best_h,best_c=b2,bc
          hs,cs=coordinate_cd(best_h,n,50,ph3,coarse=64,refine=160)
          if cs<best_c: best_h,best_c=hs,cs
    ph4=t0+0.82*budget
    if ph4>deadline: ph4=deadline
    if time.time()<ph4-20:
       hs,cs=subgradient_local(best_h,n,ph4,max_trials=700,rng=rng)
       if cs<best_c: best_h,best_c=hs,cs
       hs,cs=coordinate_cd(best_h,n,60,ph4,coarse=64,refine=180)
       if cs<best_c: best_h,best_c=hs,cs
    ph5=t0+0.95*budget
    if ph5>deadline: ph5=deadline
    if time.time()<ph5-25:
       sigmas=(0.02,0.05,0.09)
       for sgm in sigmas:
           if time.time() > ph5-30: break
           hp=project_exact(best_h + rng.normal(0.0, sgm, n), target)
           hc,cc=coordinate_cd(hp,n,35,ph5-20,coarse=48,refine=128)
           if cc < best_c:
               hs,cs=subgradient_local(hc,n,ph5-10,max_trials=150,rng=rng)
               if cs < cc:
                   hc,cc=hs,cs
               hc,cc=coordinate_cd(hc,n,35,ph5-10,coarse=56,refine=160)
               if cc < best_c:
                   best_h,best_c=hc,cc
       if time.time()<ph5-50:
          for kind in range(4):
             if time.time() > ph5-35: break
             hs=structured_start(n,kind,rng)
             hc,cc=coordinate_cd(hs,n,30,ph5-20,coarse=48,refine=128)
             if cc < best_c:
                hs2,cs2=subgradient_local(hc,n,ph5-10,max_trials=100,rng=rng)
                if cs2 < cc:
                   hc,cc=hs2,cs2
                hc,cc=coordinate_cd(hc,n,30,ph5-10,coarse=56,refine=160)
                if cc < best_c:
                   best_h,best_c=hc,cc
    # final polish
    best_h,best_c=coordinate_cd(best_h,n,80,deadline,coarse=80,refine=256)
    if time.time()<deadline-3:
       hs,cs=subgradient_local(best_h,n,deadline-1,max_trials=120,rng=rng)
       if cs<best_c:
          best_h,best_c=hs,cs
          best_h,best_c=coordinate_cd(best_h,n,30,deadline,coarse=96,refine=280)
    best_h=project_exact(best_h,target)
    best_c=compute_c5(best_h,n)
    return best_h,best_c,n
```
Potential issue: `coordinate_cd` at final with deadline may not stop if no improvement? It breaks one pass no improvement, good. If improves many, maybe to deadline. okay.

Maybe too many phases but if no improvements, each coordinate_cd one pass. Subgradient if no improvements stalls after 3. Binary may run 30k trials if improves many? It stops after noimp>10000. If noimp 10000 break. Good.

But binary_improve's `apply_delta` calls `coord_derivative` each flip, which does vector ops. n=500, M avg ~167 length, not 300; 30k*2*167*several ops ~10M. okay.

```python
import numpy as np
import time

_OFFSET_CACHE = (None, None)

def compute_c5(h, n):
    dx = 2.0 / n
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    return float(np.max(c) * dx)

def project_exact(h, target):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).ravel().copy()
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    s = float(h.sum())
    if abs(s - target) < 1e-13:
        return h
    if s > target:
        lo = 0.0
        hi = 1.0
        for _ in range(40):
            mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
            val = float(np.maximum(h - mid, 0.0).sum())
            if val > target:
                lo = mid
            else:
                hi = mid
        lam = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        h = np.maximum(h - lam, 0.0)
    else:
        lo = 0.0
        hi = 1.0
        for _ in range(40):
            mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
            val = float(np.minimum(h + mid, 1.0).sum())
            if val < target:
                lo = mid
            else:
                hi = mid
        lam = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        h = np.minimum(h + lam, 1.0)
    diff = target - float(h.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
        if diff > 0:
            mask = h < 1.0
        else:
            mask = h > 0.0
        cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
        if cnt > 0:
            h[mask] += diff / cnt
            h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
        diff = target - float(h.sum())
        if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
            if diff > 0:
                idx = int(np.argmin(1.0 - h))
                h[idx] = min(1.0, h[idx] + diff)
            else:
                idx = int(np.argmax(h))
                h[idx] = max(0.0, h[idx] + diff)
    return h

def get_offsets(n):
    global _OFFSET_CACHE
    if _OFFSET_CACHE[0] == n:
        return _OFFSET_CACHE[1]
    q = n - 1
    infos = []
    for p in range(n):
        M = p if p > n - 1 - p else n - 1 - p
        m = np.arange(-M, M + 1, dtype=int)
        pos = m >= 0
        neg = m < 0
        ab = -m
        lo = q - M
        hi = q + M
        infos.append((lo, hi, m, pos, neg, ab, M))
    _OFFSET_CACHE = (n, infos)
    return infos

def coord_derivative(h, p, n, info):
    lo, hi, m, pos, neg, ab, diag = info
    L = hi - lo + 1
    a = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
    if np.any(pos):
        idx = p + m
        valid = pos & (idx < n)
        if np.any(valid):
            a[valid] -= h[idx[valid]]
        idx1 = p - m
        valid1 = pos & (idx1 >= 0)
        if np.any(valid1):
            a[valid1] += 1.0 - h[idx1[valid1]]
    if np.any(neg):
        idxf = p + ab
        validf = neg & (idxf < n)
        if np.any(validf):
            a[validf] += 1.0 - h[idxf[validf]]
        idxs = p - ab
        valids = neg & (p >= ab)
        if np.any(valids):
            a[valids] -= h[idxs[valids]]
    return a

def apply_delta(h, c, p, delta, n, info):
    lo, hi, m, pos, neg, ab, diag = info
    h0 = h[p]
    hn = min(1.0, max(0.0, h0 + delta))
    actual = hn - h0
    if abs(actual) < 1e-15:
        return float(np.max(c))
    a = coord_derivative(h, p, n, info)
    c[lo:hi + 1] += actual * a
    q = n - 1
    c[q] -= actual * actual
    h[p] = hn
    return float(np.max(c))

def coord_step(h, c, p, n, info, current_max, coarse, refine):
    lo, hi, m, pos, neg, ab, diag = info
    base = c[lo:hi + 1]
    unaff = 0.0
    if lo > 0:
        unaff = float(np.max(c[:lo]))
    if hi < 2 * n - 2:
        v = float(np.max(c[hi + 1:]))
        if v > unaff:
            unaff = v
    if current_max < unaff:
        current_max = unaff
    a = coord_derivative(h, p, n, info)
    h0 = h[p]
    lo_d = -h0
    hi_d = 1.0 - h0
    span = hi_d - lo_d
    if span < 1e-14:
        return 0.0, current_max
    D = np.linspace(lo_d, hi_d, coarse)
    vals = base[None, :] + D[:, None] * a
    vals[:, diag] -= D * D
    row = vals.max(axis=1)
    if unaff > 0.0:
        row = np.maximum(row, unaff)
    bi = int(np.argmin(row))
    best_val = float(row[bi])
    best_d = float(D[bi])
    if best_val < current_max - 1e-12:
        cell = span / max(1, coarse - 1)
        rad = max(cell * 2.0, span / 100.0)
        rlo = max(lo_d, best_d - rad)
        rhi = min(hi_d, best_d + rad)
        if rhi > rlo + 1e-15:
            D2 = np.linspace(rlo, rhi, refine)
            vals2 = base[None, :] + D2[:, None] * a
            vals2[:, diag] -= D2 * D2
            row2 = vals2.max(axis=1)
            row2 = np.maximum(row2, unaff)
            bi2 = int(np.argmin(row2))
            if row2[bi2] < best_val - 1e-13:
                best_val = float(row2[bi2])
                best_d = float(D2[bi2])
    if abs(best_d) > 1e-14 and best_val < current_max - 1e-12:
        hn = min(1.0, max(0.0, h0 + best_d))
        actual = hn - h0
        if abs(actual) > 1e-14:
            c[lo:hi + 1] += actual * a
            q = n - 1
            c[q] -= actual * actual
            h[p] = hn
        return actual, best_val
    return 0.0, current_max

def coordinate_cd(h, n, max_passes, deadline, coarse=48, refine=144, max_drift=5.0):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).ravel().copy()
    infos = get_offsets(n)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    dx = 2.0 / n
    target = n / 2.0
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = float(np.max(c) * dx)
    for _ in range(max_passes):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
        current_max = float(np.max(c))
        drift = 0.0
        improved = False
        for p in range(n):
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break
            d, new_max = coord_step(h, c, p, n, infos[p], current_max, coarse, refine)
            if d != 0.0:
                improved = True
                current_max = new_max
                drift += d
                if abs(drift) > max_drift:
                    h = project_exact(h, target)
                    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
                    current_max = float(np.max(c))
                    drift = 0.0
        if improved:
            h = project_exact(h, target)
            c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
            nc = float(np.max(c) * dx)
            if nc < best_c - 1e-14:
                best_c = nc
                best_h = h.copy()
        else:
            break
    return best_h, best_c

def add_lag_grad(out, s, h, n):
    q = n - 1
    m = int(s) - q
    if m >= 0:
        L = n - m
        if L <= 0:
            return
        out[:L] -= h[m:m + L]
        out[m:m + L] += 1.0 - h[:L]
    else:
        a = -m
        L = n - a
        if L <= 0:
            return
        out[:L] += 1.0 - h[a:a + L]
        out[a:a + L] -= h[:L]

def topk_indices(scores, K):
    K = int(K)
    if K <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=int)
    if K >= scores.size:
        return np.arange(scores.size, dtype=int)
    return np.argpartition(scores, -K)[-K:].astype(int)

def select_indices(c, K, mode, q, rng):
    if mode == 'pos':
        sc = c[q + 1:]
        off = q + 1
    elif mode == 'neg':
        sc = c[:q]
        off = 0
    else:
        sc = c
        off = 0
    K = min(int(K), sc.size)
    if K <= 0:
        return None
    Kb = K if mode != 'rand' else min(3 * K, sc.size)
    idx = topk_indices(sc, Kb)
    if idx.size == 0:
        return None
    idx = idx + off
    if mode == 'rand' and idx.size > K:
        sel = rng.choice(idx.size, K, replace=False)
        idx = idx[sel]
    return idx

def line_search_dir(h_base, d, n, Cbase):
    best_h = h_base
    best_c = Cbase
    d = np.asarray(d, dtype=float).ravel().copy()
    d -= np.mean(d)
    nm = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
    if nm < 1e-14:
        return best_h, best_c
    d /= nm
    ts = (0.08, 0.04, 0.02, 0.01, 0.004, 0.002)
    target = n / 2.0
    for t in ts:
        ht = project_exact(h_base + t * d, target)
        ch = compute_c5(ht, n)
        if ch < best_c:
            best_h = ht
            best_c = ch
    return best_h, best_c

def subgradient_local(h, n, deadline, max_trials=1000, rng=None):
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.RandomState(12345)
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).ravel().copy()
    dx = 2.0 / n
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    best_c = float(np.max(c) * dx)
    best_h = h.copy()
    q = n - 1
    modes = ((3, 'all'), (3, 'pos'), (3, 'neg'), (10, 'all'),
             (10, 'pos'), (10, 'rand'), (25, 'all'), (25, 'rand'))
    stall = 0
    for it in range(max_trials):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
        C = float(np.max(c) * dx)
        if C < best_c - 1e-14:
            best_c = C
            best_h = h.copy()
        trial_best = None
        trial_C = C
        for K, mode in modes:
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break
            idx = select_indices(c, K, mode, q, rng)
            if idx is None or idx.size == 0:
                continue
            g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
            for sidx in idx:
                add_lag_grad(g, int(sidx), h, n)
            g /= float(idx.size)
            h2, C2 = line_search_dir(h, -g, n, C)
            if C2 < trial_C - 1e-12:
                trial_C = C2
                trial_best = h2
        if trial_best is not None and trial_C < C - 1e-12:
            h = trial_best
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall >= 3:
                break
    cf = compute_c5(h, n)
    if cf < best_c:
        best_c = cf
        best_h = h.copy()
    return best_h, best_c

def binarize(h, n):
    b = np.zeros(n)
    target = n // 2
    if target > 0 and target < n:
        order = np.argsort(h, kind='mergesort')
        b[order[-target:]] = 1.0
    return b

def binary_improve(b, n, max_trials, deadline, rng):
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=float).ravel().copy()
    infos = get_offsets(n)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    c = np.correlate(b, 1.0 - b, mode='full')
    best_units = float(np.max(c))
    best_h = b.copy()
    ones = [i for i in range(n) if b[i] > 0.5]
    zeros = [i for i in range(n) if b[i] <= 0.5]
    if not ones or not zeros:
        return best_h, best_units * dx
    noimp = 0
    for t in range(max_trials):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        i = ones[int(rng.randint(len(ones)))]
        j = zeros[int(rng.randint(len(zeros)))]
        if i == j:
            continue
        apply_delta(b, c, i, -1.0, n, infos[i])
        apply_delta(b, c, j, +1.0, n, infos[j])
        val = float(np.max(c))
        if val < best_units - 1e-12:
            best_units = val
            best_h = b.copy()
            ones.remove(i)
            zeros.append(i)
            zeros.remove(j)
            ones.append(j)
            noimp = 0
        else:
            apply_delta(b, c, j, -1.0, n, infos[j])
            apply_delta(b, c, i, +1.0, n, infos[i])
            noimp += 1
            if noimp > 10000:
                break
        if t % 1000 == 0:
            c = np.correlate(b, 1.0 - b, mode='full')
    cf = float(np.max(np.correlate(b, 1.0 - b, mode='full')) * dx)
    if cf < best_units * dx:
        best_units = cf
        best_h = b.copy()
    return best_h, best_units * dx

def structured_start(n, kind, rng):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n, endpoint=False)
    if kind == 0:
        h = 0.5 + 0.45 * np.sin(np.pi * x) + 0.05 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x)
    elif kind == 1:
        h = 0.5 + 0.45 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x) - 0.1 * np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * x)
    elif kind == 2:
        h = 0.5 + 0.45 * np.cos(np.pi * x)
    elif kind == 3:
        h = 0.5 + 0.35 * np.sin(3.0 * np.pi * x) + 0.15 * np.sin(np.pi * x)
    else:
        h = rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0, n)
    return project_exact(h, n / 2.0)

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    budget = float(budget_s)
    if budget > 60:
        deadline = t0 + budget - 0.03 * budget
    else:
        deadline = t0 + 0.9 * budget
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
        h = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).ravel().copy()
    except Exception:
        h = None
    if h is None or h.size == 0:
        n = 300
        h = structured_start(n, 0, rng)
    else:
        n = int(h.size)
        if n > 800:
            n2 = 500
            x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n, endpoint=False)
            x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n2, endpoint=False)
            h = project_exact(np.interp(x1, x0, h), n2 / 2.0)
            n = n2
        if n % 2 == 1:
            n2 = max(2, n - 1)
            x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n, endpoint=False)
            x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n2, endpoint=False)
            h = project_exact(np.interp(x1, x0, h), n2 / 2.0)
            n = n2
    target = n / 2.0
    h = project_exact(h, target)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(h, n)

    ph1 = min(t0 + 0.35 * budget, deadline)
    best_h, best_c = coordinate_cd(best_h, n, 150, ph1, coarse=48, refine=144)

    ph2 = min(t0 + 0.55 * budget, deadline)
    if time.time() < ph2 - 2:
        hs, cs = subgradient_local(best_h, n, ph2, max_trials=900, rng=rng)
        if cs < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = hs, cs
        hs, cs = coordinate_cd(best_h, n, 70, ph2, coarse=64, refine=176)
        if cs < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = hs, cs

    ph3 = min(t0 + 0.70 * budget, deadline)
    if n % 2 == 0 and time.time() < ph3 - 30:
        b = binarize(best_h, n)
        bc0 = compute_c5(b, n)
        if bc0 < best_c + 0.05:
            b2, bc2 = binary_improve(b, n, 30000, ph3, rng)
            if bc2 < best_c:
                best_h, best_c = b2, bc2
                hs, cs = coordinate_cd(best_h, n, 60, ph3, coarse=64, refine=176)
                if cs < best_c:
                    best_h, best_c = hs, cs

    ph4 = min(t0 + 0.85 * budget, deadline)
    if time.time() < ph4 - 20:
        hs, cs = subgradient_local(best_h, n, ph4, max_trials=800, rng=rng)
        if cs < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = hs, cs
        hs, cs = coordinate_cd(best_h, n, 80, ph4, coarse=72, refine=192)
        if cs < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = hs, cs

    ph5 = min(t0 + 0.95 * budget, deadline)
    if time.time() < ph5 - 25:
        for sgm in (0.02, 0.05, 0.09):
            if time.time() > ph5 - 30:
                break
            hp = project_exact(best_h + rng.normal(0.0, sgm, n), target)
            c0 = compute_c5(hp, n)
            if c0 >= best_c + 0.03:
                continue
            hc, cc = coordinate_cd(hp, n, 35, ph5 - 20, coarse=48, refine=128)
            if cc < best_c:
                hs, cs = subgradient_local(hc, n, ph5 - 10, max_trials=160, rng=rng)
                if cs < cc:
                    hc, cc = hs, cs
                hc, cc = coordinate_cd(hc, n, 35, ph5 - 10, coarse=64, refine=160)
                if cc < best_c:
                    best_h, best_c = hc, cc
        if time.time() < ph5 - 50:
            for kind in range(4):
                if time.time() > ph5 - 35:
                    break
                hs = structured_start(n, kind, rng)
                if compute_c5(hs, n) >= best_c + 0.02:
                    continue
                hc, cc = coordinate_cd(hs, n, 30, ph5 - 20, coarse=48, refine=128)
                if cc < best_c:
                    hs2, cs2 = subgradient_local(hc, n, ph5 - 10, max_trials=120, rng=rng)
                    if cs2 < cc:
                        hc, cc = hs2, cs2
                    hc, cc = coordinate_cd(hc, n, 30, ph5 - 10, coarse=64, refine=160)
                    if cc < best_c:
                        best_h, best_c = hc, cc

    best_h, best_c = coordinate_cd(best_h, n, 90, deadline, coarse=80, refine=256)
    if time.time() < deadline - 3:
        hs, cs = subgradient_local(best_h, n, deadline - 1, max_trials=130, rng=rng)
        if cs < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = hs, cs
            best_h, best_c = coordinate_cd(best_h, n, 35, deadline, coarse=96, refine=280)

    best_h = project_exact(best_h, target)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, n)
    return best_h, best_c, n
```
